## Error Response

If the Access Token is invalid, expired, or the DPoP proof fails, Corppass returns a JSON response containing an error code and description.

{% hint style="warning" %}
If the request lacks authentication altogether (e.g., no `Authorization` header or unsupported scheme), **Corppass will return HTTP 401 without including any error code, description, or body.**

This prevents revealing information about authentication requirements to unauthenticated or potentially malicious clients, as recommended by [RFC 6750 Section 3.1.](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6750.html#section-3.1)
{% endhint %}

### Response Header

The response will always include the `WWW-Authenticate` header when the access token is missing or invalid. For DPoP-bound tokens, this header specifically indicates issues with the token or its associated proof.

```http
WWW-Authenticate: DPoP error="invalid_dpop_proof", error_description="DPoP proof iat is not recent enough"
```

### Response Body

<table><thead><tr><th width="214.66796875">Field</th><th width="136.328125">Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>error</code></td><td>String</td><td><p>A standardised error code identifying the type of error that occurred.</p><p><br>See <a href="#error-codes">Error Codes</a> below for a complete list of possible values.</p></td></tr><tr><td><code>error_description</code></td><td>String</td><td>A human-readable text description providing additional details about the error.</td></tr></tbody></table>

#### Error Codes

<table><thead><tr><th width="194.41796875">Error Code</th><th width="144.0234375">HTTP Status</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>invalid_request</td><td>400</td><td><p>This may be due to one of the following:</p><ul><li>A required request parameter is missing.</li><li>A request parameter is malformed or does not meet the expected format.</li><li>The client’s JWKS endpoint is not reachable.</li><li>The JWKS object is empty or the encryption key is not available.</li><li>The TLS cipher suite used for the connection is not compliant with <a href="https://openid.bitbucket.io/fapi/fapi-2_0-baseline.html#section-4.2">FAPI 2.0 security requirements</a>.</li></ul><p>Please ensure that:</p><ul><li>All required parameters are provided and correctly formatted.</li><li>Your JWKS endpoint is accessible and returns a valid key set that complies with the required <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/technical-concepts/client-jwks">specification</a>.</li></ul></td></tr><tr><td>invalid_client</td><td>401</td><td><p>This may be due to one of the following:</p><ul><li>Missing client credential</li><li>Invalid client credential</li><li>Expired client credential</li><li>Improperly formatted client credential or assertion (e.g., malformed JWT)</li><li>The client’s JWKS does not contain the required signing key</li></ul><p>Please ensure that:</p><ul><li>The client credential is present, valid, and not expired</li><li>Your JWKS endpoint is accessible and returns a valid key set that complies with the required <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/technical-concepts/client-jwks">specification</a>.</li></ul></td></tr><tr><td>invalid_token</td><td>401</td><td>Access token is expired, invalid, or not properly bound.</td></tr><tr><td>invalid_dpop_proof</td><td>401</td><td><p>This may due to the one of the following:</p><ul><li>The DPoP proof is expired.</li><li>The DPoP proof is malformed.</li><li>The DPoP proof failed signature verification.</li><li>The DPoP proof contains missing or invalid required claims.</li></ul><p>Please ensure that:</p><ul><li>The DPoP proof is correctly structured and signed using the client's private key.</li><li>The DPoP proof complies with the required <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/technical-concepts/demonstrating-proof-of-possession-dpop">specification</a>.</li></ul></td></tr></tbody></table>

### Sample Response

<pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json"><strong>{
</strong>  "error": "invalid_dpop_proof",
  "error_description": "DPoP proof iat is not recent enough"
}
</code></pre>


# Auth Info

The `auth_info` claim provides **authorization details** about the **user acting on behalf of the entity**.

{% hint style="info" %}
This claim is only returned in the [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) response when **`authinfo`** scope is requested.
{% endhint %}

## `auth_info` Attributes

<table data-full-width="true"><thead><tr><th width="184.78125">Field</th><th width="149.86328125">Data Type</th><th width="99.79296875">Length</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>Result_Set</code></td><td>JSON</td><td>-</td><td>Digital Service authorization result set.</td></tr></tbody></table>

#### `auth_info.Result_Set` attributes

<table data-full-width="true"><thead><tr><th width="184.8515625">Field</th><th width="149.8828125">Data Type</th><th width="100.21875">Length</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>ESrvc_Row_Count</code></td><td>Integer</td><td>10</td><td><p>The number of Digital Services for which the authorizations are present in the <code>Result_Set.ESrvc_Result</code> JSON object.</p><p>Returns <code>0</code> if <code>ESrvc_Result</code> is empty.</p></td></tr><tr><td><code>ESrvc_Result</code></td><td>Array of JSON</td><td>-</td><td><p>Array of JSON objects, each containing authorization details for the Digital Service.</p><p>Refer to <a href="#e-service-item-attributes-auth_info.result_set.esrvc_result-item">E-Service item attributes</a> for more details.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

#### E-Service item attributes (`auth_info.Result_Set.ESrvc_Result[]` item)

Contains authorization details for the digital service.

<table data-full-width="true"><thead><tr><th width="179.8515625">Field</th><th width="150.265625">Data Type</th><th width="99.93359375">Length</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>CPESrvcID</code></td><td>String</td><td>25</td><td>The readable ID of the Digital Service.</td></tr><tr><td><code>Auth_Result_Set</code></td><td>JSON</td><td>-</td><td><p>JSON object. Contains user assignment details for the Digital Service.</p><p>Refer to <a href="#user-assignment-result-set-attributes-auth_info.result_set.esrvc_result-.auth_result_set">User Assignment Result Set attributes</a> for more details.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

#### User Assignment Result Set attributes (`auth_info.Result_Set.ESrvc_Result[].Auth_Result_Set`)

<table data-full-width="true"><thead><tr><th width="180.28515625">Field</th><th width="150.21484375">Data Type</th><th width="99.8359375">Length</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>Row_Count</code></td><td>Integer</td><td>10</td><td><p>The number of rows included in the auth result set (ie. number of assignments the user has for the e-service).</p><p><br>e.g. If the user is granted two separate assignments to the same e-service on behalf of the same entity, this value will be <code>2</code> . The following result set will contain then contain two objects, each representing one assignment.</p></td></tr><tr><td><code>Row</code></td><td>Array of JSON</td><td>-</td><td><p>Array of JSON objects, where each JSON object describes a single third-party assignment.</p><p>Refer to <a href="#user-assignment-item-attributes-auth_info.result_set.esrvc_result-.auth_result_set.row-item">User Assignment Item attributes</a> for more details.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

#### User Assignment Item attributes (`auth_info.Result_Set.ESrvc_Result[].Auth_Result_Set.Row[]` item)

<table data-full-width="true"><thead><tr><th width="180.0625">Field</th><th width="149.625">Data Type</th><th width="100.125">Length</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>CPEntID_SUB</code></td><td>String</td><td>32</td><td>Sub-UEN value of that entity to which the user is assigned the authorization.<br><br>If the Digital Service requires a mandatory Sub-UEN attribute but no value was supplied, <code>"ERROR_MISSING_VALUE"</code> will be returned for the field.</td></tr><tr><td><code>CPRole</code></td><td>String</td><td>20</td><td>The role assigned to the user in this particular authorisation.</td></tr><tr><td><code>StartDate</code></td><td>String (format YYYY-MM-DD)</td><td>10</td><td>The start date for validity of the authorisation.</td></tr><tr><td><code>EndDate</code></td><td>String (format YYYY-MM-DD)</td><td>10</td><td>The end date for validity of the authorisation.</td></tr><tr><td><code>Parameter</code></td><td>Array of JSON</td><td>-</td><td><p>This is an optional array of parameters (JSON objects containing only name and value pair attributes) defined by Digital Service administrator.<br><br>These parameters will be populated as required. These parameters use a name-value pair format.<br><br>If no parameters are defined this will be returned as empty array.<br><br>If a parameter is defined as mandatory by the Digital Service administrator but no value was supplied, <code>"ERROR_MISSING_VALUE"</code> will be returned for the field.</p><p>Refer to <a href="#user-assignment-parameter-attributes-auth_info.result_set.esrvc_result-.auth_result_set.row-.paramet">User Assignment Parameter attributes</a> for more details.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

## Sample Payload

<details>

<summary>User with two authorisations</summary>

Authorization info payload for a User with two authorisations:

* One to e-service `SAMPLE-ESERVICE`
* One to e-service `OTHER-ESERVICE`

{% code lineNumbers="true" %}

```json
{
  "auth_info": {
    "Result_Set": {
      "ESrvc_Row_Count": 2,
      "ESrvc_Result": [
        {
          "CPESrvcID": "SAMPLE-ESERVICE",
          "Auth_Result_Set": {
            "Row_Count": 1,
            "Row": [
              {
                "CPEntID_SUB": "",
                "CPRole": "Approver",
                "StartDate": "2017-11-14",
                "EndDate": "9999-12-31",
                "Parameter": [
                  {
                    "name": "Effective YA",
                    "value": "2020"
                  },
                ]
              }
            ]
          }
        },
        {
          "CPESrvcID": "OTHER-ESERVICE",
          "Auth_Result_Set": {
            "Row_Count": 1,
            "Row": [
              {
                "CPEntID_SUB": "",
                "CPRole": "Editor",
                "StartDate": "2017-11-14",
                "EndDate": "9999-12-31",
                "Parameter": []
              }
            ]
          }
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}

```

{% endcode %}

</details>


# Third-party Auth Info

The `tp_auth_info` claim provides **third-party authorization details** about the **user acting on behalf of the entity**.

{% hint style="info" %}
This claim is only returned in the [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) response when

1. **`tpauthinfo`** scope is requested.
2. The digital service **supports third-party** functionality on Corppass.
3. The user has **third-party authorisations** for the digital service.
   {% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
**Note:** A user may have multiple third-party authorizations for a given Digital Service.

*Example:* For the GST Digital Service, a user from a third-party firm is authorized to file GST on behalf of 5 client entities. In this case, all 5 third-party authorizations will be returned in the `tp_auth_info` claim.
{% endhint %}

## `tp_auth_info` **Attributes**

<table data-full-width="true"><thead><tr><th width="184.78125">Field</th><th width="149.86328125">Data Type</th><th width="99.79296875">Length</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>Result_Set</code></td><td>JSON</td><td>-</td><td>Digital Service authorization result set.</td></tr></tbody></table>

#### `tp_auth_info.Result_Set` attributes

<table data-full-width="true"><thead><tr><th width="184.8515625">Field</th><th width="149.8828125">Data Type</th><th width="100.21875">Length</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>ESrvc_Row_Count</code></td><td>Integer</td><td>10</td><td><p>The number of Digital Services for which the authorizations are present in the <code>Result_Set.ESrvc_Result</code> JSON object.</p><p>For third-party authorisations, this value will always be set to <code>1</code>.</p></td></tr><tr><td><code>ESrvc_Result</code></td><td>Array of JSON</td><td>-</td><td><p>Array of JSON objects, each containing third-party authorization details for the Digital Service.</p><p>For third-party authorisations, there will only be a single JSON object.</p><p>Refer to <a href="#e-service-item-attributes-tp_auth_info.result_set.esrvc_result-item">E-Service item attributes</a> for more details.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

#### E-Service item attributes (`tp_auth_info.Result_Set.ESrvc_Result[]` item)

Contains authorization details for the digital service.

Contains third-party assignment details for the Digital Service.</p><p>Refer to <a href="#third-party-assignment-result-set-attributes-tp_auth_info.result_set.esrvc_result-.auth_set">Third-Party Assignment Result Set attributes</a> for more details.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

The possible values for entity type are:</p><ul><li>UEN</li><li>NON-UEN (Includes non-UEN entities as well as ASGD/ITR entities)</li><li>GSTN</li></ul></td></tr><tr><td><code>Auth_Result_Set</code></td><td>Array of JSON</td><td>-</td><td><p>JSON object. {

```json
                "CP_ClntEnt_TYPE": "UEN",
                "Auth_Result_Set": {
                  "Row_Count": 1,
                  "Row": [
                    {
                      "CP_ClntEnt_SUB": "",
                      "CPRole": "Maker",
                      "StartDate": "2025-09-05",
                      "EndDate": "9999-12-31",
                      "Parameter": []
                    }
                  ]
                }
              },
              {
                "CP_Clnt_ID": "T99BB0000A",
                "CP_ClntEnt_TYPE": "UEN",
                "Auth_Result_Set": {
                  "Row_Count": 1,
                  "Row": [
                    {
                      "CP_ClntEnt_SUB": "",

                      "StartDate": "2025-09-05",
                      "EndDate": "9999-12-31",
                      "Parameter": []
                    }
                  ]
                }
              }
            ]
          }
        }
      ]

}
```

{% endcode %}

# Entity Info

The `entity_info` claim provides **business-related information** about the **entity the user is transacting on behalf of**.

{% hint style="info" %}
This claim is only returned in the [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) response when

1. [**Myinfo Business entity scopes**](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/entity-scopes) are requested.
2. The digital service is a [**Myinfo Business application**](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/products/myinfo-business).
   {% endhint %}

## `entity_info` Attributes

Attributes in `entity_info` are scope-driven.

Each attribute maps directly to a requested [Entity Scope](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/entity-scopes) and will only be included in the response if that scope is present.

For the full set of attributes and their structures, refer to the **OpenAPI specifications**:

{% file src="/files/lXMe7LqYQSE4LGboBJ16" %}

### Common Attribute Patterns

Depending on the type of data, attributes may follow one of the patterns below:

**Pattern A: Metadata + Value**

Used when the field contains a single value.

```json
{
  "classification": "C",
  "source": "1",
  "lastupdated": "2019-03-26",
  "value": "2018-01-31"
}
```

**Pattern B: Metadata + Code + Description**

Used for coded values with a human-readable description.

```json
{
  "classification": "C",
  "source": "1",
  "lastupdated": "2019-03-26",
  "code": "LC",
  "desc": "Local Company"
}
```

**Pattern C: Metadata at Parent Level, Value in Child Fields**

Used when multiple related values share the same metadata.

```json
{
  "appointment_date": {
    "value": "2018-01-31"
  },
  "classification": "C",
  "source": "1",
  "lastupdated": "2019-03-26"
}
```

**Pattern D: Metadata at Parent Level, Code/Desc in Child Fields**

Used when child fields contain coded values.

```json
{
 "position": {
    "code": "23",
    "desc": "Partner"
  },
  "classification": "C",
  "source": "1",
  "lastupdated": "2019-01-01"
}
```

**Pattern E: Unavailable Data**

Used when data is not available from the source.

```json
{
  "classification": "C",
  "source": "1",
  "lastupdated": "2019-03-26",
  "unavailable": true
}
```

**Note:**

* Not all attributes follow the same structure.
* The exact structure depends on the type of data being returned.
* Developers should refer to the OpenAPI specification for the complete schema definition.

## Sample Payload

<details>

<summary><code>entity_info.basic_profile.name</code> attribute for the scope <code>entity.basic_profile.name</code></summary>

```json
{
  "entity_info": {
    "basic_profile": {
      "name": {
        "classification": "C",
        "lastupdated": "2018-01-31",
        "source": "1",
        "value": "ABC LL"
      }
    }
  }
}
```

</details>


# Person Info

The `person_info` claim provides **personal information** about the **user acting on behalf of the entity**.

{% hint style="info" %}
This claim is only returned in the [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) response when

1. [**Myinfo Business person scopes**](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/person-scopes) are requested.
2. The digital service is a [**Myinfo Business application**](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/products/myinfo-business).
   {% endhint %}

## `person_info` Attributes

Attributes in `person_info` are scope-driven.

Each attribute maps directly to a requested [Person Scope](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/person-scopes) and will only be included in the response if that scope is present.

For the full set of attributes and their structures, refer to the **OpenAPI specifications**:

{% file src="/files/lXMe7LqYQSE4LGboBJ16" %}

Refer to [common attribute patterns](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/entity-info#common-attribute-patterns) for some of the common attribute patterns.

## Sample Payload

<details>

<summary><code>person_info.name</code> attribute for the scope <code>user.name</code></summary>

```json
{
  "person_info": {
    "name": {
       "classification": "C",
       "lastupdated": "2019-03-26",
       "source": "1",
       "value": "TAN XIAO HUI"
    }
  }
}
```

</details>


# Corppass Info

The `corppass_info` claim provides information about the **transacting user’s Corppass account**.

{% hint style="info" %}
This claim is only returned in the [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) response when

1. [**Myinfo Business Corppass scopes**](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/corppass-scopes) are requested.
2. The digital service is a [**Myinfo Business application**](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/products/myinfo-business).
   {% endhint %}

## `corppass_info` Attributes

<table data-full-width="true"><thead><tr><th width="184.78125">Field</th><th width="149.86328125">Data Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>email</code></td><td>String</td><td><p>Corppass registered email address.</p><p><em>Required scope:</em> <code>corppass.email</code></p></td></tr><tr><td><code>email_verified</code></td><td>Boolean</td><td><p>Indicates if the Corppass registered email has been verified.</p><p><em>Required scope:</em> <code>corppass.email</code></p></td></tr></tbody></table>

## Sample Payload

<details>

<summary><code>email</code> attributes for the scope <code>corppass.email</code></summary>

```json
{
  "corppass_info": {
    "email": "john@corppass.gov.sg",
    "email_verified": true
  }
}
```

</details>


# Supported TLS Cipher Suites

In line with the enhanced security requirements of FAPI 2.0, only the following cipher suites are supported:

| TLS Version | Allowed Ciphers                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| TLS v1.3    | All ciphers                                                                                                                                                   |
| TLS v1.2    | <ul><li>ECDHE-RSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256</li><li>ECDHE-RSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384</li><li>ECDHE-ECDSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256</li><li>ECDHE-ECDSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384</li></ul> |


# Scopes

Scopes define the level of access a Relying Party (RP) is requesting from Corppass during an authorization request. Each scope corresponds to a specific set of claims or datasets that the RP may access.

Once the user authorises the request, the claims associated with the requested scopes are returned either in ID token (via the [Token Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/3.-token-endpoint)) and/or through the [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint), depending on the type of digital service application.

## Usage

When initiating an authorization request via , RPs specify the required scopes in the `scope` parameter. Multiple scopes are space-separated.

Example:

```
scope=openid authinfo user.identity entity.identity entity.basic_profile.name
```

Refer to [Pushed Authorization Request (PAR)](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/1.-pushed-authorization-request-par-endpoint) for detailed specifications on constructing the request.

information about the transacting user.</li></ul><p><em>Available to all Corppass applications.</em></p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top"><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes">Myinfo Business Scopes</a></td><td valign="top"><p>Myinfo Business scopes provide access to entity and personal information about the acting user and the entity they represent.</p><p>These include:</p><ul><li><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/entity-scopes">Entity scopes</a>, providing access to business-related information about the entity the user is transacting on behalf of.</li><li><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/person-scopes">Person scopes</a>, providing access to personal information about the transacting user.</li><li><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/corppass-scopes">Corppass scopes</a>, providing access to information about the transacting user's Corppass account.</li></ul><p><em>Available to only Myinfo Business applications.</em></p></td></tr></tbody></table>


# Corppass Standard Scopes

Corppass standard scopes provide access to identity information about the transacting user, and the entity that the user is transacting on behalf of.

These scopes are **available to all Corppass applications**.

## Protocol Scopes

Protocol scopes are mandatory scopes required for authentication and authorization.

<table><thead><tr><th width="199.6171875" valign="top">Scope</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top"><code>openid</code></td><td><p><strong>Mandatory</strong> scope to indicate the request is an OpenID Connect flow.</p><p>The scope must be included in the <code>scope</code> parameter of an authorization request.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

## Authorization Scopes

Authorization scopes provide access to authorization details about the transacting user.

<table><thead><tr><th width="200.38671875" valign="top">Scope</th><th width="360.30859375" valign="top">Description</th><th valign="top">Data Access</th><th valign="top">Endpoint</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top"><code>authinfo</code></td><td valign="top">View authorization details about the transacting user.</td><td valign="top"><pre><code>auth_info
</code></pre></td><td valign="top"><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint">UserInfo</a></td></tr><tr><td valign="top"><code>tpauthinfo</code></td><td valign="top">View third-party authorization details about the transacting user.</td><td valign="top"><pre><code>tp_auth_info
</code></pre></td><td valign="top"><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint">UserInfo</a></td></tr></tbody></table>

## Entity Scopes

Entity scopes provide access to profile information about the entity that the user is transacting on behalf of.

<table><thead><tr><th width="200.08984375" valign="top">Scope</th><th width="360.4921875" valign="top">Description &#x26; Endpoint</th><th valign="top">Data Access</th><th valign="top">Endpoint</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top"><code>entity.identity</code></td><td valign="top"><p>View entity identity, includes:</p><ul><li>entity type</li><li>registration number</li><li>country of incorporation using ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 standard<br>Refer to <a href="https://www.iso.org/iso-3166-country-codes.html">ISO 3166</a> for possible values.</li></ul></td><td valign="top"><pre><code><strong>sub_attributes:
</strong>- entity_type
- entity_reg_number
- entity_coi
</code></pre></td><td valign="top"><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/3.-token-endpoint">Token</a></td></tr><tr><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.name</code></td><td valign="top">View entity name.</td><td valign="top"><pre><code>sub_attributes:
- entity_name
</code></pre></td><td valign="top"><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/3.-token-endpoint">Token</a></td></tr><tr><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.uen_status</code></td><td valign="top">View entity UEN status.</td><td valign="top"><pre><code>sub_attributes:
- entity_uen_status
</code></pre></td><td valign="top"><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/3.-token-endpoint">Token</a></td></tr></tbody></table>

## User Scopes

User scopes provide access to profile information about the transacting user.

<table><thead><tr><th width="199.69921875" valign="top">Scope</th><th width="360.08984375" valign="top">Description</th><th valign="top">Data Access</th><th valign="top">Endpoint</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top"><code>user.identity</code></td><td valign="top"><p>View acting user's identity, includes:</p><ul><li>account type</li><li>identity number</li><li>country of issuance using ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 standard<br>Refer to <a href="https://www.iso.org/iso-3166-country-codes.html">ISO 3166</a> for possible values.</li></ul></td><td valign="top"><pre><code>act.sub_attributes:
- account_type
- identity_number
- identity_coi
</code></pre></td><td valign="top"><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/3.-token-endpoint">Token</a></td></tr><tr><td valign="top"><code>user.name</code></td><td valign="top">View acting user's name.</td><td valign="top"><pre><code>act.sub_attributes:
- name
</code></pre></td><td valign="top"><a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/3.-token-endpoint">Token</a></td></tr></tbody></table>


# Myinfo Business Scopes

Myinfo Business scopes provide access to entity and personal information about the acting user and the entity they represent.

These scopes are **only available to** [**Myinfo Business applications**](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/products/myinfo-business).

Corresponding Myinfo Business data is returned via the [**Userinfo Endpoint**](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) as:

* `entity_info` for [Myinfo Business entity scopes](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/entity-scopes), with the **`entity.`** prefix
* `person_info` for [Myinfo Business person scopes](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/person-scopes), with the **`user.`** prefix
* `corppass_info` for [Myinfo Business Corppass scopes](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes/myinfo-business-scopes/corppass-scopes), with the **`corppass.`** prefix

#### Example

<table><thead><tr><th width="276.6796875">Scope</th><th>Data returned in Userinfo Response</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong><code>entity.</code></strong><code>basic_profile.name</code></td><td><p><strong><code>entity_info.</code></strong><code>basic_profile.name</code></p><pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json">{
  "entity_info": {
    "basic_profile": {
      "name": {
        "classification": "C",
        "lastupdated": "2018-01-31",
        "source": "1",
        "value": "ABC LL"
      }
    }
  }
}
</code></pre></td></tr><tr><td><strong><code>user.</code></strong><code>name</code></td><td><p><strong><code>person_info.</code></strong><code>name</code></p><pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json">{
  "person_info": {
    "name": {
       "classification": "C",
       "lastupdated": "2019-03-26",
       "source": "1",
       "value": "TAN XIAO HUI"
    }
  }
}
</code></pre></td></tr><tr><td><strong><code>corppass.</code></strong><code>email</code></td><td><p><strong><code>corppass_info.</code></strong><code>email</code></p><p><strong><code>corppass_info.</code></strong><code>email_verified</code></p><pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json">{
  "corppass_info": {
    "email": "john@corppass.gov.sg",
    "email_verified": true
  }
}
</code></pre></td></tr></tbody></table>


# Entity Scopes

Myinfo Business entity scopes provide access to **business-related information** about the **entity the user is transacting on behalf of**.

These scopes are only available to [Myinfo Business applications](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/products/myinfo-business).

The corresponding data is returned via the [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) under `entity_info`. Refer to [Entity Info](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/entity-info) for more details.

## Basic Profile

<table><thead><tr><th width="214.703125" valign="top">Name</th><th width="259.81640625" valign="top">Scope</th><th valign="top">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top">Company Type</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.company_type</code></td><td valign="top">Type of company.<br>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/entity-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Constitution</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.constitution</code></td><td valign="top">Constitution of Business or Public Accounting Firms.<br>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/entity-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Country of Incorporation</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.country_of_incorporation</code></td><td valign="top">Country of incorporation using ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 standard. Refer to <a href="https://www.iso.org/iso-3166-country-codes.html">ISO 3166</a> for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Expiry Date</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.expiry_date</code></td><td valign="top">Expiry date in "YYYY-MM-DD" format.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Name</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.name</code></td><td valign="top">Entity's registered name.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Primary Activity</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.primary_activity</code></td><td valign="top">Primary activity using Singapore Standard Industrial Classification (SSIC) 2025 edition.<br>Refer to <a href="https://www.singstat.gov.sg/standard-classifications/national-classifications/singapore-standard-industrial-classification-ssic">Singapore Standard Industrial Classification (SSIC)</a> for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Registration Date</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.registration_date</code></td><td valign="top">Registration date in "YYYY-MM-DD" format.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Registration Number</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.registration_number</code></td><td valign="top">Unique Entity Number (UEN).</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Secondary Activity</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.secondary_activity</code></td><td valign="top">Secondary activity using Singapore Standard Industrial Classification (SSIC) 2025 edition.<br>Refer to <a href="https://www.singstat.gov.sg/standard-classifications/national-classifications/singapore-standard-industrial-classification-ssic">Singapore Standard Industrial Classification (SSIC)</a> for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Type</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.type</code></td><td valign="top"><p>Type of entity.</p><p>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/entity-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values. If this field is LC or FC, refer to <code>company_type</code>. If this field is BN or PF, refer to <code>constitution</code>.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">UEN Status</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.basic_profile.uen_status</code></td><td valign="top"><p>UEN registration status.</p><p>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/entity-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><thead><tr><th width="215.0390625" valign="top">Name</th><th width="259.66015625" valign="top">Scope</th><th valign="top">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top">Address</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.address</code></td><td valign="top">Entity's latest registered address details.</td></tr></tbody></table>

Revenue</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.financials.group_financial.revenue</code></td><td valign="top"><p>Monetary value for group revenue in the fiscal period.</p><p>Refer to <code>currency</code> for the currency.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Group Financial - Share Capital</td><td valign="top"><code>entity.financials.group_financial.share_capital</code></td><td valign="top"><p>Monetary value for group share capital in the fiscal period.</p><p>Refer to <code>currency</code> for the currency.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

# Person Scopes

Myinfo Business person scopes provide access to **personal information** about the **user acting on behalf of the entity**.

These scopes are only available to [Myinfo Business applications](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/products/myinfo-business).

The corresponding data is returned via the [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) under `person_info`. Refer to [Person Info](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info) for more details.

## Personal

<table><thead><tr><th width="151.22265625" valign="top">Name</th><th width="164.2734375" valign="top">Scope</th><th width="119.92578125">Available For</th><th valign="top">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top">NRIC / FIN</td><td valign="top"><code>user.uinfin</code></td><td>SC, PR, FIN</td><td valign="top">NRIC number or FIN of user. NRIC number is the unique identifier given to every Singapore Citizens (SC) and Permanent Residents (PR), while FIN is the unique identifier for Foreigners.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Alias Name</td><td valign="top"><code>user.aliasname</code></td><td>SC, PR</td><td valign="top">Refers to an alternate/additional name of the user that is legally recognised.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Country / Place of Birth</td><td valign="top"><code>user.birthcountry</code></td><td>SC, PR, FIN</td><td valign="top">Refer to <a href="https://public.cloud.myinfo.gov.sg/dpp/frontend/assets/api-lib/myinfo/downloads/myinfo-api-code-tables.xlsx">Code Listing</a> (CountryPlaceCode) for list of possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Date of Birth</td><td valign="top"><code>user.dob</code></td><td>SC, PR, FIN</td><td valign="top"></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Employment Sector</td><td valign="top"><code>user.employmentsector</code></td><td>FIN</td><td valign="top">Refers to the employment sector of a FIN holder. Only applies to a FIN holder with a valid pass issued by MOM.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Hanyu Pinyin Name</td><td valign="top"><code>user.hanyupinyinname</code></td><td>SC, PR</td><td valign="top">Refers to the officially romanised Chinese name of the user.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Hanyu Pinyin Alias Name</td><td valign="top"><code>user.hanyupinyinaliasname</code></td><td>SC, PR</td><td valign="top">Refers to the legally-recognised alternate officially romanised Chinese name of the user.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Type of Housing</td><td valign="top"><code>user.housingtype</code></td><td>SC, PR, FIN</td><td valign="top">Note that this is determined based on user's Registered Address (if it is not a HDB flat). It has no association with housing ownership.<br>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Married Name</td><td valign="top"><code>user.marriedname</code></td><td>SC, PR</td><td valign="top">Refers to the family name or surname adopted by the user upon marriage.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Marital Status</td><td valign="top"><code>user.marital</code></td><td>SC, PR</td><td valign="top"><p>Marital status is based on records that had been reasonably verified by the relevant Government agency upon receipt.</p><p>Marital Status must be made editable at digital services even though data source is MSF.</p><p>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Principal Name</td><td valign="top"><code>user.name</code></td><td>SC, PR, FIN</td><td valign="top">Full name of user printed on NRIC or FIN card. Includes Surname if any.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Nationality/Citizenship</td><td valign="top"><code>user.nationality</code></td><td>SC, PR, FIN</td><td valign="top">Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Pass Expiry Date</td><td valign="top"><code>user.passexpirydate</code></td><td>FIN</td><td valign="top"><p>Refers to the pass expiry date of a FIN holder.</p><p>Note that this only applies to a FIN holder with a valid pass issued by ICA/MOM.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Passport Expiry Date</td><td valign="top"><code>user.passportexpirydate</code></td><td>SC</td><td valign="top">Passport expiry date of the user.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Passport Number</td><td valign="top"><code>user.passportnumber</code></td><td>SC</td><td valign="top">Passport number of the user.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Pass Type</td><td valign="top"><code>user.passtype</code></td><td>FIN</td><td valign="top">Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Pass Status</td><td valign="top"><code>user.passstatus</code></td><td>FIN</td><td valign="top"><p>This refers to the pass status of a FIN holder.</p><p>Available values:</p><ul><li>Live</li><li>Approved. (Interim status in which the FIN holder has yet to receive the pass)</li></ul><p>Note that this only applies to a FIN holder with a valid pass issued by ICA/MOM.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Residential Status</td><td valign="top"><code>user.residentialstatus</code></td><td>SC, PR</td><td valign="top">Indicate if the user is a Citizen, PR or others. Blank value will be returned for FIN holder.<br>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Secondary Race</td><td valign="top"><code>user.secondaryrace</code></td><td>SC, PR</td><td valign="top"><p>Refers to secondary racial category of the Person, if any.</p><p>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Race</td><td valign="top"><code>user.race</code></td><td>SC, PR, FIN</td><td valign="top">Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Sex</td><td valign="top"><code>user.sex</code></td><td>SC, PR, FIN</td><td valign="top"><p>Gender of the user.</p><p>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Registered Address</td><td valign="top"><code>user.regadd</code></td><td>SC, PR, FIN</td><td valign="top"><p>For SC/PR - Registered address is the address that is printed on the NRIC card.</p><p>For FIN - Registered address is applicable for foreigners under the following Pass Types:</p><ul><li><p>ICA-issued Passes</p><ul><li>Long Term Visit Pass / Long Term Visit Pass +</li><li>Student Pass</li><li>Immigration Exemption Order</li></ul></li><li><p>MOM-issued Passes</p><ul><li>S Pass</li><li>Employment Pass</li><li>Personalised Employment Pass</li><li>EntrePass</li><li>Dependent Pass</li></ul></li></ul></td></tr></tbody></table>

## Property

<table><thead><tr><th width="215.1171875" valign="top">Name</th><th width="260.27734375" valign="top">Scope</th><th valign="top">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top">HDB Ownership - Address</td><td valign="top"><code>user.hdbownership.address</code></td><td valign="top">Flat Address.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">HDB Ownership - Date of Purchase</td><td valign="top"><code>user.hdbownership.dateofpurchase</code></td><td valign="top">Effective Date of flat purchase.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">HDB Ownership - Type of HDB Dwelling</td><td valign="top"><code>user.hdbownership.hdbtype</code></td><td valign="top"><p>HDB Flat Type.</p><p>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">HDB Ownership - Monthly Loan Instalment</td><td valign="top"><code>user.hdbownership.monthlyloaninstalment</code></td><td valign="top">Monthly instalment payable to HDB.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">HDB Ownership - Number of Owners</td><td valign="top"><code>user.hdbownership.noofowners</code></td><td valign="top"><p>Number of Owners.</p><p>Note that this does not include executors, administrators or trustees.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">HDB Ownership - Outstanding HDB Loan Balance</td><td valign="top"><code>user.hdbownership.outstandingloanbalance</code></td><td valign="top">Outstanding amount payable to HDB.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">HDB Ownership - Type of HDB Dwelling</td><td valign="top"><code>user.hdbtype</code></td><td valign="top"><p>HDB Flat Type.</p><p>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/person-info">OpenAPI</a> specification for possible values.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

## Finance

<table><thead><tr><th width="215.3671875" valign="top">Name</th><th width="260.18359375" valign="top">Scope</th><th valign="top">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top">CPF Contribution History (up to 15 months)</td><td valign="top"><code>user.cpfcontributions</code></td><td valign="top"><p>The Central Provident Fund is a mandatory social security savings scheme funded by contributions from employers and employees.</p><p>For each contribution record, the 4 fields (Paid for Month, Paid On, Employer Name, Contribution Total) will be provided.</p><p>Only Contribution(s) related to employment will be provided. Other contributions, such as self-employed or government top-ups, are presently excluded.</p><p>CPF will return the contributions received (or paid on) in the past 15 months up to the preceding month. Note that a member could have multiple contributions per month from the same employer.</p><ul><li><strong>Example 1:</strong><br>If the query is made in March-24, contribution provided will be for contribution received in the period from January-23 to March-24 (inclusive). This is provided that March-24 contribution had been received, else contribution provided will be January-23 to February-24.</li><li><strong>Example 2:</strong><br>Assuming a member has a late CPF contribution for work done in Nov-22 (beyond January-23 to March-24) that was credited only in Jan-23. If the query is made in March-24, contribution provided will include the late contribution as it was received in the period from January-23 to March-24. The contribution period range of January-23 to March-24 (inclusive) remains.</li></ul><p>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.developer.singpass.gov.sg/docs/products/myinfo/data-display-guidelines">CPF display guidelines</a>.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">CPF Housing Withdrawal</td><td valign="top"><code>user.cpfhousingwithdrawal</code></td><td valign="top"><p>The Central Provident Fund is a mandatory social security savings scheme funded by contributions from employers and employees.</p><p>The CPF Housing Withdrawal data include the following for the purpose of financing a HDB flat or private residential property:</p><ul><li>Address: This is the registered address of property which withdrawal is made.</li><li>Principal Withdrawal Amount: This is the CPF principal amount user has withdrawn under the CPF housing schemes.</li><li>Monthly Instalment Amount: This is the monthly CPF deduction for payment of user's property.</li><li>Accrued Interest Amount: This is the interest that user would have earned had user's savings remained in the CPF account.</li><li>Total Amount of CPF Allowed for Property: The maximum CPF savings allowed to be used for the financing the property, excluding the amount used for stamp duty and legal fees.</li></ul><p>Refer to the <a href="https://docs.developer.singpass.gov.sg/docs/products/myinfo/data-display-guidelines">CPF display guidelines</a>.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Notice of Assessment (Basic, Latest Year)</td><td valign="top"><code>user.noa-basic</code></td><td valign="top"><p>This refers to the taxpayer's total income after deducting allowable expenses and approved donations. Total income includes:</p><ul><li>Trade</li><li>Employment</li><li>Rent</li><li>Interest</li></ul><p>This field will only return:</p><ul><li>1 yearly assessable income and</li><li><p>the corresponding year value. The yearly assessable income will be the latest available income up to the last 2 Years of Assessments. If the subscriber does not have any assessment record for up to 2 back years, Myinfo will return the following:</p><ul><li>Assessable Income= 'NA' or '-'</li><li>Year Assessed='NA'.</li></ul></li></ul><p>To note:</p><ul><li>Assessable Income details is only available for finalised assessment. Thus, income information will not be available if the subscriber has a Default Assessment (e.g. taxpayers who have not submitted their tax return).</li><li>This may not be applicable for foreigners depending on their tax residency. For example, in the case of double tax treaties with other countries, foreigner may not need to pay Singapore tax.</li></ul></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Notice of Assessment (Detailed, Latest Year)</td><td valign="top"><code>user.noa</code></td><td valign="top"><p>Similar to 'Notice of Assessment (Basic, Latest Year)' data field, including the following income breakdown</p><ul><li>Employment</li><li>Trade</li><li>Rent</li><li>Interest</li></ul><p>Additional details in this data field:</p><ul><li>Tax clearance : Indicator will be 'Y' for scenario where non-Singapore Citizen employee that ceases employment in Singapore, and goes on an overseas posting or plans to leave Singapore for more than three months. Only need to display this field if the value = 'Y'.</li><li>Tax Category : Type of Income Tax Bill.<br>Refer to <a href="https://www.iras.gov.sg/taxes/individual-income-tax/basics-of-individual-income-tax/receive-tax-bill-pay-tax-check-refunds/understanding-my-tax-assessment">IRAS tax assessment</a> for details.</li></ul></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Notice of Assessment (Detailed, Last 2 Years)</td><td valign="top"><code>user.noahistory</code></td><td valign="top">Similar to 'Notice of Assessment (Basic, Latest Year)' data field, but for the last 2 years instead.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Notice of Assessment (Basic, Last 2 Years)</td><td valign="top"><code>user.noahistory-basic</code></td><td valign="top">Similar to 'Notice of Assessment (Basic, Latest Year)' data field, but for the last 2 years instead.</td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Ownership of Private Residential Property</td><td valign="top"><code>user.ownerprivate</code></td><td valign="top"><p>This refers to the ownership of private residential property.</p><p>Note that this does not include shophouses which are assessed as commercial properties.</p><p>This field will be 'Y', if the corresponding NRIC/FIN, in IRAS records, is a primarily, secondary or CC owner of an active private residential property and is required to pay property tax. Otherwise will be 'N'.</p><p>Where the NRIC/FIN is not found in IRAS records, it will be returned as 'NA'.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>

## Employment

<table><thead><tr><th width="215.3671875" valign="top">Name</th><th width="260.18359375" valign="top">Scope</th><th valign="top">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top">CPF Employers</td><td valign="top"><code>user.cpfemployers</code></td><td valign="top"><p>Employer Name for the CPF Contribution. Includes contribution month/year.</p><p>Only Contribution(s) related to employment will be provided. Other contributions, such as self-employed or government top-ups, are presently excluded.</p><p>CPF will return the contributions received (or paid on) in the past 15 months up to the preceding month. Note that a member could have multiple contributions per month from the same employer.</p></td></tr><tr><td valign="top">Name of Employer</td><td valign="top"><code>user.employment</code></td><td valign="top"><p>This refers to the employment sector of a FIN holder.</p><p>Note that this only applies to a FIN holder with a valid pass issued by MOM.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>


# Corppass Scopes

Myinfo Business Corppass scopes provide access to information about the **transacting user’s Corppass account**.

These scopes are only available to [Myinfo Business applications](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/products/myinfo-business).

The corresponding data is returned via the [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) under `corppass_info`. Refer to [Corppass Info](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint/corppass-info) for more details.

{% hint style="info" %}
In the Singpass Developer Portal, Corppass scopes can be found under the Person scopes tab when configuring your Myinfo Business application.
{% endhint %}

<table><thead><tr><th width="214.84375" valign="top">Name</th><th width="259.57421875" valign="top">Scope</th><th width="329.03515625" valign="top">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td valign="top">Corppass Registered Email</td><td valign="top"><code>corppass.email</code></td><td valign="top"><p>Corppass registered email address of the transacting user’s Corppass account.</p><p><em>Source: Corppass</em></p></td></tr></tbody></table>


# Migration Guides


# Migration Guide: Legacy to FAPI 2.0

This guide assists partners in transitioning existing integrations from the [Corppass Authorization API (Legacy)](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-legacy) to the new **FAPI 2.0‑compliant Authorization API**.

The new implementation strengthens security by adopting the **Financial-grade API (FAPI) 2.0 Security Profile**, built on OAuth 2.0 and OpenID Connect.

## Why Migrate?

FAPI 2.0 introduces enhanced security mechanisms, mandated flows, and updated token structures that improve confidentiality, integrity, and replay protection compared to the Legacy Security Profile. These changes align with global security best practices and provide long‑term reliability.

Legacy Security Profile will be deprecated, and partners must migrate to continue using the service.

***

## Key Changes

See [Changelog: FAPI 2.0 Launch](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/changelog/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0-launch)

## Migration Steps

### Step 1: Review Client Configuration & Scopes

Review your client configuration on the [**Corppass Developer Portal**](https://developer.corppass.gov.sg/) to accommodate new mandatory parameters and scope changes.

1. **Review Authentication Context Type:**\
   FAPI 2.0 introduces `authentication_context_type` as a mandatory parameter to define the transaction context.

   1. **Configuration:** Your client profile now includes a list of "Supported Authentication Context Types". Currently, only `APP_AUTHENTICATION_DEFAULT` is supported. This value will be automatically backfilled for all existing clients.
   2. **Constraint:** During an authorization request, you can only request a context type that is explicitly configured for your client. For now, you must use `APP_AUTHENTICATION_DEFAULT` value in your requests.

   Refer to [Authentication Context Parameters](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/1.-pushed-authorization-request-par-endpoint/authentication-context-parameters) section for more details.<br>
2. **Review Scopes:**\
   FAPI 2.0 introduces new finer-grained scopes.

   1. **Configuration:** To ensure continuity, Corppass will automatically backfill your client profile with finer-grained FAPI 2.0 scopes corresponding to your legacy access.
   2. **Review:** Audit the backfilled scopes in your client configuration. Remove any that provides access to data your application no longer needs.

   Refer to [Scopes](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/scopes) section for more details.
3. **Review OIDC Redirect URLs:**\
   FAPI 2.0 now mandates the use of redirect URLs with `https` scheme.
   1. **Configuration:** Make sure you have at least one usable redirect URL with `https` scheme configured.
   2. **Constraint**: During an authorization request, you must provide a redirect URL with `https` scheme, or else you will not be allowed to initialise authorization on FAPI 2.0.

### Step 2: Retrieve new metadata from OpenID discovery endpoint

The OpenID Connect Discovery URL remains the same as the Legacy API.

1. **New Endpoints Metadata:** Query the existing Discovery URL to retrieve updated provider metadata. Configure your application to use the new endpoint values found in the metadata:
   1. `pushed_authorization_request_endpoint` (New: Required for PAR)
   2. `userinfo_endpoint` (Replaces `/authorization-info`)

Refer to [OpenID Discovery Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/0.-well-known-endpoints/openid-discovery-endpoint) for more details.

### Step 3: Authorization Request

**Significant Change:** You can no longer construct the authorization URL directly in the browser. You need to implement **Pushed Authorization Request (PAR)**.

1. **Call the PAR Endpoint (`/request`):**
   1. Initiate the flow via a *back-channel `POST` request*.
   2. Include DPoP proof in the request.
   3. Include the necessary OIDC parameters (e.g., `client_id`, `scope`, `redirect_uri`) along with the new mandatory fields (e.g., `client_assertion`, `code_challenge`, `authentication_context_type`).
   4. Corppass returns a `request_uri` that references your payload.
2. **Browser Redirect to Authorization Endpoint (`/authorize`):**
   1. Redirect the user to the Authorization Endpoint using only `client_id` and the returned `request_uri` returned in the previous step.
   2. This replaces the Legacy method of exposing sensitive parameters directly in the browser URL.

Refer to [Pushed Authorization Request (PAR) Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/1.-pushed-authorization-request-par-endpoint) for more details.

### Step 4: Token Exchange

The Token Endpoint (`/token`) now enforces strict security proofs.

1. **Prepare the Request:**
   1. **Mandatory Client Authentication:** Use `client_assertion` (Private Key JWT).
   2. **Mandatory PKCE:** Include the `code_verifier` matching the `code_challenge` from PAR.
   3. **Mandatory DPoP:** Include a `DPoP` HTTP header. The public key in this header will be bound to the resulting Access Token.
2. **Handle the Response:**
   1. **Access Token:** Store the opaque token for subsequent API calls where needed.
      1. Note: The token is sender-constrained; you must use the same DPoP private key from the request to sign the DPoP header when using this token later.
   2. **ID Token:** Decrypt the JWE using your private encryption key to retrieve the signed payload, then validate the signature. See Step 5.

Refer to [Token Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/3.-token-endpoint) for more details.

### Step 5: Decrypt & Map ID Token

The ID Token is an Encrypted JWT (JWE).

1. **Decrypt:** Use your private encryption key (from Step 2) to decrypt the token.
2. **Validate:** Verify the signature of the decrypted payload using Corppass's public signing key.
3. **Map New Claims:** Update your parsing logic to the new hierarchical structure.

<table data-full-width="true"><thead><tr><th width="127.703125">Data</th><th>Legacy Claim</th><th>FAPI 2.0 Claim</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Entity Details</td><td><p><code>entityInfo.*</code></p><pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json">{
  "entityInfo": {
    "CPEntID": "T09LL0001B",
    "CPEnt_TYPE": "UEN",
    "CPEnt_Status": "Registered",
    "CPNonUEN_Country": "",
    "CPNonUEN_RegNo": "",
    "CPNonUEN_Name": ""
  }
} 
</code></pre></td><td><p><code>sub</code>, <code>sub_attributes.*</code></p><pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json">{
  "sub": "T09LL0001B",
  "sub_type": "entity",
  "sub_attributes": {
    "entity_type": "UEN",
    "entity_reg_number": "T09LL0001B",
    "entity_coi": "SG",
    "entity_name": "My Example Company",
    "entity_uen_status": "Registered"
  }
}
</code></pre></td></tr><tr><td>User Details</td><td><p><code>sub</code></p><pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json">{
  "sub": "s=S1234567P,uuid=0f14a2fc-09c2-4780-95f0-8c28347f2780,u=CP192,c=SG"
} 
</code></pre><p><code>userInfo.*</code></p><pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json">{
  "userInfo":{
    "CPAccType": "User",
    "CPUID_FullName": "John Grisham",
    "ISSPHOLDER": "YES"
  }
}
</code></pre></td><td><p><code>act.*</code></p><pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json">{
  "act": {
    "sub": "0f14a2fc-09c2-4780-95f0-8c28347f2780",
    "sub_type": "user",
    "sub_attributes": {
      "account_type": "standard", 
      "identity_number": "S1234567P",
      "identity_coi": "SG",
      "name": "John Grisham"
    }
  }
}
</code></pre></td></tr></tbody></table>

Refer to [ID Token](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/3.-token-endpoint/id-token) section for more details.

### Step 6: Userinfo Endpoint

This is applicable if your application fetches additional user / authorization data.

1. **Use the new userinfo URL**
   1. In the legacy API, this was available on the `/authorization-info` endpoint.
   2. In the FAPI 2.0 API, this is served on the `/userinfo` endpoint (the metadata retrieved in Step 2, under `userinfo_endpoint`).
2. **Update Authorization header prefix to be DPoP instead of Bearer**
   1. In the legacy API, the Authorization header would have been sent as a bearer token:

      ```
      Authorization: Bearer <your_access_token>
      ```
   2. In the FAPI 2.0 API, this needs to be sent with a `DPoP` prefix:

      ```
      Authorization: DPoP <your_access_token>
      ```
3. **Handle new response format**
   1. Update your JSON parser to handle snake\_case keys (e.g., `auth_info` instead of `AuthInfo`).
   2. Since the response is now returned as a JWE, you must decrypt the payload using your registered private key before processing the claims.

Refer to [Userinfo Endpoint](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide/4.-userinfo-endpoint) for more details.

### Step 7: Error Handling

Ensure your application handles standard OAuth 2.0 error parameters (`error`, `error_description`, `state`):

1. **Handle Redirect URI:** Error query parameters on the callback URL (e.g., `https://client.com/cb?error=access_denied&error_description=...`)
2. **Handle API Response:** JSON bodies for `/request`, `/token` , or `userinfo` failures.

Refer to the respective endpoints for more details.


# Migration Guide: Myinfo Business v1/v2 to v3 (FAPI 2.0)

This guide assists partners in transitioning existing integrations from Myinfo Business v2 (Legacy) to Myinfo Business v3.

Myinfo Business v3 is built on the [Corppass Authorization API (FAPI 2.0)](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0), which adopts the Financial-grade API (FAPI) 2.0 Security Profile based on OAuth 2.0 and OpenID Connect.

## Why Migrate?

FAPI 2.0 introduces enhanced security mechanisms, mandated flows, and updated token structures that improve confidentiality, integrity, and replay protection compared to the Legacy APIs. These changes align with global security best practices and provide long‑term reliability.

All legacy Myinfo Business v1/v2 applications will be deprecated, and partners must migrate to continue using the service.

***

## Key Changes

***

## Scope Changes

### Entity Information Updates

* Renamed `uen` to `registration_number`. This change affects basic profile, entity appointments, and shareholders.
* Replaced `entity-status` with `uen-status` (support for `entity-status` has been dropped).
* Dropped the `ownership` field from basic profile.
* Dropped `primary user described activity` and `secondary user described-activity`.
* Introduced a new `id_type` (identification type) field for individual appointments and shareholders.
* Dropped support for `corppass-email` and `corppass-mobileno` on individual appointments and shareholders.
* Deprecated the Government contracts category (`gov-contracts`).

Please expand the section below for a detailed mapping of the entity object changes

***

## Migration Steps

### Step 1: Create Myinfo Business v3 Application

Create a new Myinfo Business v3 application on the [Singpass Developer Portal (SDP)](https://developer.singpass.gov.sg/). Existing Client ID / App ID cannot be used in the new version as they are not interchangeable.

Refer to [Getting Started](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/products/myinfo-business/getting-started) guide for step-by-step instructions on creating and configuring your application.

### Step 2: Implement Myinfo Business v3 Integration

Myinfo Business v3 is built on the [Corppass Authorization API (FAPI 2.0)](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0) and introduces significant changes to the authorization flow, security requirements, and data handling.

As the APIs and integration patterns differ substantially from v1/v2, partners are required to implement the integration based on the latest specifications.

Refer to [Integration Guide](https://docs.corppass.gov.sg/technical-specifications/corppass-authorization-api-fapi-2.0/integration-guide) for full implementation details.
